Every threshold comparison with an age on one side. "Anchored" means the age is taken on the determination date or month whatever month is being determined; "per month" means the item is determined month by month (person-month scope) or a per-month item reads it, so a retroactive month, an application month before the determination month, or a later month is judged with the determination date's age.

## 2. Branch coverage

A branch is a `case` arm (`else` included), each side of an `otherwise` (the fact stated, the default taken), and each outcome of a threshold comparison. "Rule" counts what the rule tests reach, "household" what the stored households reach, "either" both together. "At boundary" counts comparisons some test evaluates with both sides equal.

## 3. Mutation score

Each threshold comparison flipped between strict and inclusive, and each `case` of two or more arms with its first two results swapped. Killed: some test that reaches the node and passes on the ledger fails on the mutant (at most 150 reaching tests are run per mutant). Unreached: no test evaluates the node.

| Program | Comparison flips | killed | unreached | Arm swaps | killed | unreached | Score (killed / all) | Score (killed / reached) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| AdultFinancial | 93 | 34 | 3 | 74 | 71 | 0 | 63% | 64% |
| All | 25 | 5 | 1 | 6 | 6 | 0 | 35% | 37% |
| CCCAP | 33 | 15 | 0 | 62 | 62 | 0 | 81% | 81% |
| CHP+ | 44 | 8 | 2 | 28 | 27 | 0 | 49% | 50% |
| ColoradoWorks | 63 | 30 | 2 | 50 | 49 | 0 | 70% | 71% |
| LEAP | 26 | 11 | 0 | 26 | 26 | 0 | 71% | 71% |
| Medicaid | 233 | 64 | 5 | 160 | 148 | 0 | 54% | 55% |
| SNAP | 156 | 62 | 3 | 128 | 127 | 0 | 67% | 67% |
| SunBucks | 13 | 5 | 0 | 6 | 6 | 0 | 58% | 58% |
| **all** | 686 | 234 | 16 | 540 | 522 | 0 | 62% | 62% |

## 4. Generated boundary cases

686 threshold comparisons; 366 generated. The threshold T is the comparison's fixed side as evaluated in the reaching test; "lands" says which neighbour the value at T gives the same answer as, for the comparison itself, the item that holds it, and the reaching test's own identifier. "Wording" is the phrase the cited excerpts put next to T's figure, and the side it puts T on.
